General topic ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\tisGeneral := update.ThreadID == nil || *update.ThreadID == generalTopicID\n\tif isGeneral {\n\t\tif update.Content != nil && update.Content.IsCommand() && r.OnCommand != nil {\n\t\t\tgroup, err := r.db.GetGroup(ctx, update.ChatID)\n\t\t\tif err != nil {\n\t\t\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] get group for chat %d: %v\", update.ChatID, err)\n\t\t\t\treturn\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t// Publish command event (Phase 6 format)\n\t\t\tif r.eventPublisher != nil {\n\t\t\t\tcommand, args := update.Content.ExtractCommandAndArgs()\n\t\t\t\tusername := \"\"\n\t\t\t\tif update.FromUser.Username != nil {\n\t\t\t\t\tusername = *update.FromUser.Username\n\t\t\t\t}\n\t\t\t\tr.eventPublisher.PublishCommand(\n\t\t\t\t\tupdate.ChatID,\n\t\t\t\t\tcommand,\n\t\t\t\t\targs,\n\t\t\t\t\t\"General\",\n\t\t\t\t\tevents.FormatUsername(username, update.FromUser.FirstName),\n\t\t\t\t\t\"ok\",\n\t\t\t\t)\n\t\t\t}\n\t\t\tr.OnCommand(ctx, update, group)\n\t\t}\n\t\t// Non-command messages in General topic are silently ignored.\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// ── 6. Named topic ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\ttid := *update.ThreadID\n\n\t// Publish message received event (Phase 6 format)\n\tif r.eventPublisher != nil && update.Content != nil {\n\t\tusername := \"\"\n\t\tif update.FromUser.Username != nil {\n\t\t\tusername = *update.FromUser.Username\n\t\t}\n\t\tuser := events.FormatUsername(username, update.FromUser.FirstName)\n\n\t\t// Extract preview from text content\n\t\tpreview := \"\"\n\t\tif update.Content.Text != nil {\n\t\t\tpreview = events.TruncatePreview(*update.Content.Text, 100)\n\t\t} else if update.Content.Caption != nil {\n\t\t\tpreview = events.TruncatePreview(*update.Content.Caption, 100)\n\t\t} else {\n\t\t\tpreview = update.Content.Type\n\t\t}\n\n\t\tr.eventPublisher.PublishMessageIn(\n\t\t\tupdate.ChatID,\n\t\t\ttid,\n\t\t\tevents.FormatTopicID(tid),\n\t\t\tuser,\n\t\t\tpreview,\n\t\t)\n\t}\n\n\tsession, err := r.db.GetSession(ctx, update.ChatID, tid)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] get session for (%d, %d): %v\", update.ChatID, tid, err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\tif session != nil {\n\t\t// Existing session — dispatch directly.\n\t\tif r.OnSession != nil {\n\t\t\tr.OnSession(ctx, update, session, nil)\n\t\t}\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\n\t// No session — check whether this group is registered before creating one.\n\tgroup, err := r.db.GetGroup(ctx, update.ChatID)\n\tif err != nil {\n\t\tlog.Printf(\"[router] get group for chat %d: %v\", update.ChatID, err)\n\t\treturn\n\t}\n\tif group == nil {\n\t\treturn // unregistered group — silently ignore\n\t}\n\n\t// Registered group, new topic — let the session handler create the session.\n\tif r.OnSession != nil {\n\t\tr.OnSession(ctx, update, nil, group)\n\t}\n}\n","numLines":284,"startLine":1,"totalLines":284}}}

s.mu.Unlock()\n155\t\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d:%d\", chatID, threadID)\n156\t\tsession := s.sessions[key]\n157\t\tif session == nil {\n158\t\t\tsession = &SessionInfo{\n159\t\t\t\tChatID:     chatID,\n160\t\t\t\tThreadID:   threadID,\n161\t\t\t\tSessionID:  sessionID,\n162\t\t\t\tLastActive: time.Now(),\n163\t\t\t}\n164\t\t\ts.sessions[key] = session\n165\t\t}\n166\t\tsession.Model = model\n167\t\tsession.Status = status\n168\t\tsession.NotificationMode = notifMode\n169\t\tsession.CWD = cwd\n170\t\tsession.MessageCount = msgCount\n171\t\tsession.TotalCostUSD = cost\n172\t\tsession.LastActive = time.Now()\n173\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n174\t}\n175\t\n176\t// RemoveSession removes a session from the state.\n177\tfunc (s *State) RemoveSession(chatID, threadID int64) {\n178\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n179\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n180\t\tkey := fmt.Sprintf(\"%d:%d\", chatID, threadID)\n181\t\tdelete(s.sessions, key)\n182\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n183\t}\n184\t\n185\t// UpdateHealth updates the health status.\n186\tfunc (s *State) UpdateHealth(healthy bool, checks []HealthCheck) {\n187\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n188\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n189\t\ts.health.Healthy = healthy\n190\t\ts.health.Checks = checks\n191\t\ts.health.Timestamp = time.Now()\n192\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n193\t}\n194\t\n195\t// GetSessions returns a copy of the current sessions.\n196\tfunc (s *State) GetSessions() []*SessionInfo {\n197\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n198\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n199\t\tsessions := make([]*SessionInfo, 0, len(s.sessions))\n200\t\tfor _, sess := range s.sessions {\n201\t\t\tsessions = append(sessions, sess)\n202\t\t}\n203\t\treturn sessions\n204\t}\n205\t\n206\t// GetHealth returns a copy of the health status.\n207\tfunc (s *State) GetHealth() HealthStatus {\n208\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n209\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n210\t\treturn s.health\n211\t}\n212\t\n213\t// GetMessageLog returns a copy of the message log.\n214\tfunc (s *State) GetMessageLog() []LogEntry {\n215\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n216\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n217\t\tlog := make([]LogEntry, len(s.messageLog))\n218\t\tcopy(log, s.messageLog)\n219\t\treturn log\n220\t}\n221\t\n222\t// GetCommandLog returns a copy of the command log.\n223\tfunc (s *State) GetCommandLog() []CommandEntry {\n224\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n225\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n226\t\tlog := make([]CommandEntry, len(s.commandLog))\n227\t\tcopy(log, s.commandLog)\n228\t\treturn log\n229\t}\n230\t\n231\t// GetTotalCost returns the total cost across all sessions.\n232\tfunc (s *State) GetTotalCost() float64 {\n233\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n234\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n235\t\ttotal := 0.0\n236\t\tfor _, sess := range s.sessions {\n237\t\t\ttotal += sess.TotalCostUSD\n238\t\t}\n239\t\treturn total\n240\t}\n241\t\n242\t// GetActiveMessageCount returns the count of active messages.\n243\tfunc (s *State) GetActiveMessageCount() int {\n244\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n245\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n246\t\treturn s.activeMessageCount\n247\t}\n248\t\n249\t// IncrementActiveMessageCount increments the active message count.\n250\tfunc (s *State) IncrementActiveMessageCount() {\n251\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n252\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n253\t\ts.activeMessageCount++\n254\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n255\t}\n256\t\n257\t// DecrementActiveMessageCount decrements the active message count.\n258\tfunc (s *State) DecrementActiveMessageCount() {\n259\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n260\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n261\t\tif s.activeMessageCount > 0 {\n262\t\t\ts.activeMessageCount--\n263\t\t}\n264\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n265\t}\n266\t\n267\t// AddCostEntry adds a cost entry with timestamp.\n268\tfunc (s *State) AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID int64, costUSD float64, model string) {\n269\t\ts.mu.Lock()\n270\t\tdefer s.mu.Unlock()\n271\t\tentry := CostEntry{\n272\t\t\tTime:     time.Now(),\n273\t\t\tChatID:   chatID,\n274\t\t\tThreadID: threadID,\n275\t\t\tCostUSD:  costUSD,\n276\t\t\tModel:    model,\n277\t\t}\n278\t\ts.costLog = append(s.costLog, entry)\n279\t\tif len(s.costLog) > maxCostEntries {\n280\t\t\ts.costLog = s.costLog[1:]\n281\t\t}\n282\t\ts.lastUpdate = time.Now()\n283\t}\n284\t\n285\t// GetCostToday returns the total cost for today (UTC).\n286\tfunc (s *State) GetCostToday() float64 {\n287\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n288\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n289\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n290\t\ttodayStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), 0, 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n291\t\ttotal := 0.0\n292\t\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n293\t\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(todayStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(todayStart) {\n294\t\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n295\t\t\t}\n296\t\t}\n297\t\treturn total\n298\t}\n299\t\n300\t// GetCostThisHour returns the total cost for the current hour (UTC).\n301\tfunc (s *State) GetCostThisHour() float64 {\n302\t\ts.mu.RLock()\n303\t\tdefer s.mu.RUnlock()\n304\t\tnow := time.Now().UTC()\n305\t\thourStart := time.Date(now.Year(), now.Month(), now.Day(), now.Hour(), 0, 0, 0, time.UTC)\n306\t\ttotal := 0.0\n307\t\tfor _, entry := range s.costLog {\n308\t\t\tif entry.Time.UTC().After(hourStart) || entry.Time.UTC().Equal(hourStart) {\n309\t\t\t\ttotal += entry.CostUSD\n310\t\t\t}\n311\t\t}\n312\t\treturn total\n313\t}\n314\t\n315\t// tickMsg is sent periodically to update the UI.\n316\ttype tickMsg time.Time\n317\t\n318\t// eventReceivedMsg is sent when an event is received from the socket.\n319\ttype eventReceivedMsg Event\n320\t\n321\t// connectedMsg is sent when successfully connected to the event socket.\n322\ttype connectedMsg struct {\n323\t\tConn net.Conn\n324\t}\n325\t\n326\t// disconnectedMsg is sent when disconnected from the event socket.\n327\ttype disconnectedMsg struct {\n328\t\terr error\n329\t}\n330\t\n331\t// eventWithConnMsg carries an event along with its connection.\n332\ttype eventWithConnMsg struct {\n333\t\tEvent Event\n334\t\tConn  net.Conn\n335\t}\n336\t\n337\t// model is the Bubble Tea model for the dashboard.\n338\ttype model struct {\n339\t\tstate          *State\n340\t\tconn           net.Conn\n341\t\tsocketPath     string\n342\t\treconnectDelay time.Duration\n343\t\twidth          int\n344\t\theight         int\n345\t\tconnected      bool\n346\t\tlastError      error\n347\t\tquitting       bool\n348\t}\n349\t\n350\t// initialModel creates the initial model.\n351\tfunc initialModel(socketPath string) model {\n352\t\treturn model{\n353\t\t\tstate:          NewState(),\n354\t\t\tsocketPath:     socketPath,\n355\t\t\treconnectDelay: time.Second,\n356\t\t\twidth:          80,\n357\t\t\theight:         24,\n358\t\t\tconnected:      false,\n359\t\t}\n360\t}\n361\t\n362\t// Init initializes the model.\n363\tfunc (m model) Init() tea.Cmd {\n364\t\treturn tea.Batch(\n365\t\t\tm.connectCmd(),\n366\t\t\ttickCmd(),\n367\t\t)\n368\t}\n369\t\n370\t// Update handles messages and updates the model.\n371\tfunc (m model) Update(msg tea.Msg) (tea.Model, tea.Cmd) {\n372\t\tswitch msg := msg.(type) {\n373\t\tcase tea.KeyMsg:\n374\t\t\tswitch msg.String() {\n375\t\t\tcase \"q\", \"ctrl+c\":\n376\t\t\t\tm.quitting = true\n377\t\t\t\treturn m, tea.Quit\n378\t\t\tcase \"r\":\n379\t\t\t\t// Force reconnect\n380\t\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n381\t\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n382\t\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n383\t\t\t\t}\n384\t\t\t\tm.connected = false\n385\t\t\t\treturn m, m.connectCmd()\n386\t\t\t}\n387\t\tcase connectedMsg:\n388\t\t\tm.connected = true\n389\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n390\t\t\tm.reconnectDelay = time.Second\n391\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n392\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n393\t\t\t}\n394\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n395\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n396\t\tcase disconnectedMsg:\n397\t\t\tm.connected = false\n398\t\t\tm.lastError = msg.err\n399\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n400\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n401\t\t\t\tm.conn = nil\n402\t\t\t}\n403\t\t\treturn m, tea.Tick(m.reconnectDelay, func(t time.Time) tea.Msg {\n404\t\t\t\treturn connectedMsg{}\n405\t\t\t})\n406\t\tcase eventWithConnMsg:\n407\t\t\t// Store the connection and handle the event\n408\t\t\tif m.conn != nil {\n409\t\t\t\tm.conn.Close()\n410\t\t\t}\n411\t\t\tm.conn = msg.Conn\n412\t\t\tm.connected = true\n413\t\t\tm.lastError = nil\n414\t\t\tm.handleEvent(msg.Event)\n415\t\t\treturn m, waitForNextEvent(msg.Conn)\n416\t\tcase eventReceivedMsg:\n417\t\t\tm.handleEvent(Event(msg))\n418\t\t\treturn m, m.readEventsCmd()\n419\t\tcase tickMsg:\n420\t\t\treturn m, tickCmd()\n421\t\tcase tea.WindowSizeMsg:\n422\t\t\tm.width = msg.Width\n423\t\t\tm.height = msg.Height\n424\t\t\treturn m, nil\n425\t\t}\n426\t\treturn m, nil\n427\t}\n428\t\n429\t// getFloat64 extracts a float64 value from an event field.\n430\tfunc getFloat64(event Event, key string) float64 {\n431\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n432\t\t\tswitch val := v.(type) {\n433\t\t\tcase float64:\n434\t\t\t\treturn val\n435\t\t\tcase int:\n436\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n437\t\t\tcase int64:\n438\t\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n439\t\t\t}\n440\t\t}\n441\t\treturn 0\n442\t}\n443\t\n444\t// getInt64 extracts an int64 value from an event field.\n445\tfunc getInt64(event Event, key string) int64 {\n446\t\treturn int64(getFloat64(event, key))\n447\t}\n448\t\n449\t// getString extracts a string value from an event field.\n450\tfunc getString(event Event, key string) string {\n451\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n452\t\t\tif s, ok := v.(string); ok {\n453\t\t\t\treturn s\n454\t\t\t}\n455\t\t}\n456\t\treturn \"\"\n457\t}\n458\t\n459\t// getBool extracts a bool value from an event field.\n460\tfunc getBool(event Event, key string) bool {\n461\t\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n462\t\t\tif b, ok := v.(bool); ok {\n463\t\t\t\treturn b\n464\t\t\t}\n465\t\t}\n466\t\treturn false\n467\t}\n468\t\n469\t// handleEvent processes a single event from the stream.\n470\tfunc (m *model) handleEvent(event Event) {\n471\t\teventType := getString(event, \"type\")\n472\t\n473\t\tswitch eventType {\n474\t\t// Phase 6 Event Types\n475\t\tcase \"message_in\":\n476\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n477\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n478\t\t\tpreview := getString(event, \"preview\")\n479\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN %s %s: %s\", topic, user, preview))\n480\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n481\t\n482\t\tcase \"message_out\":\n483\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n484\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n485\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n486\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n487\t\t\ttokens := int(getFloat64(event, \"tokens\"))\n488\t\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n489\t\n490\t\t\tif status == \"streaming\" {\n491\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s streaming %d tokens\", topic, tokens))\n492\t\t\t} else if status == \"complete\" {\n493\t\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, \"\")\n494\t\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT %s done %d tokens $%.4f\", topic, tokens, costUSD))\n495\t\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n496\t\t\t}\n497\t\n498\t\tcase \"command\":\n499\t\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n500\t\t\targs := getString(event, \"args\")\n501\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n502\t\t\tuser := getString(event, \"user\")\n503\t\t\tresult := getString(event, \"result\")\n504\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command+\" \"+args, 0, result == \"ok\")\n505\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD %s %s %s %s → %s\", topic, user, command, args, result))\n506\t\n507\t\tcase \"session_update\":\n508\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n509\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n510\t\t\ttopic := getString(event, \"topic\")\n511\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n512\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n513\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, \"\", model, status, \"live\", \"\", 0, 0)\n514\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"SESSION %s %s: %s (%s)\", topic, status, model, status))\n515\t\n516\t\tcase \"health\":\n517\t\t\tproxyOK := getBool(event, \"proxy_ok\")\n518\t\t\tdbOK := getBool(event, \"db_ok\")\n519\t\t\tproxyLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"proxy_latency_ms\")\n520\t\t\tdbLatencyMs := getInt64(event, \"db_latency_ms\")\n521\t\n522\t\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{\n523\t\t\t\t{Name: \"proxy\", Healthy: proxyOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", proxyLatencyMs)},\n524\t\t\t\t{Name: \"database\", Healthy: dbOK, Message: fmt.Sprintf(\"%dms\", dbLatencyMs)},\n525\t\t\t}\n526\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(proxyOK && dbOK, checks)\n527\t\n528\t\t// Legacy Event Types (for backward compatibility)\n529\t\tcase \"session_created\", \"session_updated\":\n530\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n531\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n532\t\t\tsessionID := getString(event, \"session_id\")\n533\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n534\t\t\tstatus := getString(event, \"status\")\n535\t\t\tnotifMode := getString(event, \"notification_mode\")\n536\t\t\tif notifMode == \"\" {\n537\t\t\t\tnotifMode = \"live\"\n538\t\t\t}\n539\t\t\tcwd := getString(event, \"cwd\")\n540\t\t\tmsgCount := int(getFloat64(event, \"message_count\"))\n541\t\t\tcost := getFloat64(event, \"total_cost_usd\")\n542\t\t\tm.state.UpdateSession(chatID, threadID, sessionID, model, status, notifMode, cwd, msgCount, cost)\n543\t\n544\t\tcase \"session_closed\":\n545\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n546\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n547\t\t\tm.state.RemoveSession(chatID, threadID)\n548\t\n549\t\tcase \"message_received\":\n550\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n551\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n552\t\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n553\t\t\tcontentType := getString(event, \"content_type\")\n554\t\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n555\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG IN ch=%d th=%d msg=%d type=%s uid=%d\", chatID, threadID, messageID, contentType, userID))\n556\t\t\tm.state.IncrementActiveMessageCount()\n557\t\n558\t\tcase \"message_sent\":\n559\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n560\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n561\t\t\tmessageID := getInt64(event, \"message_id\")\n562\t\t\tpurpose := getString(event, \"purpose\")\n563\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"MSG OUT ch=%d th=%d msg=%d purp=%s\", chatID, threadID, messageID, purpose))\n564\t\t\tm.state.DecrementActiveMessageCount()\n565\t\n566\t\tcase \"command_executed\":\n567\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n568\t\t\tcommand := getString(event, \"command\")\n569\t\t\tuserID := getInt64(event, \"user_id\")\n570\t\t\tsuccess := getBool(event, \"success\")\n571\t\t\tm.state.AddCommandEntry(command, userID, success)\n572\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"CMD ch=%d %s uid=%d ok=%t\", chatID, command, userID, success))\n573\t\n574\t\tcase \"cost_recorded\":\n575\t\t\tchatID := getInt64(event, \"chat_id\")\n576\t\t\tthreadID := getInt64(event, \"thread_id\")\n577\t\t\tcostUSD := getFloat64(event, \"cost_usd\")\n578\t\t\tmodel := getString(event, \"model\")\n579\t\t\tm.state.AddCostEntry(chatID, threadID, costUSD, model)\n580\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"COST ch=%d th=%d $%.4f %s\", chatID, threadID, costUSD, model))\n581\t\n582\t\tcase \"health_check\":\n583\t\t\thealthy := getBool(event, \"healthy\")\n584\t\t\tchecks := []HealthCheck{}\n585\t\t\tif c, ok := event[\"checks\"].([]interface{}); ok {\n586\t\t\t\tfor _, checkItem := range c {\n587\t\t\t\t\tif checkMap, ok := checkItem.(map[string]interface{}); ok {\n588\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck := HealthCheck{}\n589\t\t\t\t\t\tif name, ok := checkMap[\"name\"].(string); ok {\n590\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Name = name\n591\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n592\t\t\t\t\t\tif h, ok := checkMap[\"healthy\"].(bool); ok {\n593\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Healthy = h\n594\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n595\t\t\t\t\t\tif msg, ok := checkMap[\"message\"].(string); ok {\n596\t\t\t\t\t\t\tcheck.Message = msg\n597\t\t\t\t\t\t}\n598\t\t\t\t\t\tchecks = append(checks, check)\n599\t\t\t\t\t}\n600\t\t\t\t}\n601\t\t\t}\n602\t\t\tm.state.UpdateHealth(healthy, checks)\n603\t\n604\t\tcase \"system_error\":\n605\t\t\tcomponent := getString(event, \"component\")\n606\t\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n607\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"ERROR [%s] %s\", component, message))\n608\t\n609\t\tcase \"system_info\":\n610\t\t\tmessage := getString(event, \"message\")\n611\t\t\tm.state.AddLogEntry(fmt.Sprintf(\"INFO %s\", message))\n612\t\t}\n613\t}\n614\t\n615\t// View renders the UI.\n616\tfunc (m model) View() string {\n617\t\tif m.quitting {\n618\t\t\treturn \"Goodbye!\\n\"\n619\t\t}\n620\t\n621\t\t// Define styles\n622\t\ttitleStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n623\t\t\tBold(true).\n624\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"86\")). // Light blue\n625\t\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n626\t\n627\t\tpanelStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n628\t\t\tBorder(lipgloss.RoundedBorder()).\n629\t\t\tBorderForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"238\")). // Grey\n630\t\t\tPadding(0, 1)\n631\t\n632\t\theaderStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().\n633\t\t\tBold(true).\n634\t\t\tForeground(lipgloss.Color(\"228\")). // Yellow\n635\t\t\tMarginBottom(1)\n636\t\n637\t\tsuccessStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"142\")) // Green\n638\t\terrorStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"203\"))   // Red\n639\t\twarningStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"229\")) // Yellow\n640\t\tdimStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Foreground(lipgloss.Color(\"241\"))    // Grey\n641\t\tstatusStyle := lipgloss.NewStyle().Bold(true)                       // Bold for status\n642\t\n643\t\t// Calculate layout\n644\t\tleftWidth := m.width / 2\n645\t\trightWidth := m.width - leftWidth - 4 // 4 for borders/gaps\n646\t\n647\t\tsessionHeight := m.height / 2\n648\t\thealthHeight := 6\n649\t\tlogHeight := m.height - sessionHeight - healthHeight - 4 // 4 for headers/gaps\n650\t\n651\t\t// Header\n652\t\theader := titleStyle.Render(\"Telegram Claude Bridge Dashboard\")\n653\t\tif m.connected {\n654\t\t\theader += successStyle.Render(\" ● Connected\")\n655\t\t} else {\n656\t\t\theader += errorStyle.Render(\" ● Disconnected\")\n657\t\t\tif m.lastError != nil {\n658\t\t\t\theader += dimStyle.Render(fmt.Sprintf(\" (%s)\", m.lastError))\n659\t\t\t}\n660\t\t}\n661\t\theader += float64(val)\n\t\tcase int64:\n\t\t\treturn float64(val)\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\treturn 0\n}\n\n// getInt64 extracts an int64 value from an event field.\nfunc getInt64(event Event, key string) int64 {\n\treturn int64(getFloat64(event, key))\n}\n\n// getString extracts a string value from an event field.\nfunc getString(event Event, key string) string {\n\tif v, ok := event[key]; ok {\n\t\tif s,
